Peak must exceed liquidus by 20°C\n}\n\n\ndef max_slope_in_temp_band(df_tc, tmin, tmax):\n    \"\"\"Calculate maximum slope within temperature band for a single TC\"\"\"\n    g = df_tc.sort_values(\"time_s\")\n    t = g[\"time_s\"].to_numpy(dtype=float)\n    y = g[\"temp_c\"].to_numpy(dtype=float)\n    best = None\n    for i in range(1, len(g)):\n        dt = t[i] - t[i-1]\n        if dt <= 0:\n            continue\n        # Both endpoints must be within the band\n        if (tmin <= y[i-1] <= tmax) and (tmin <= y[i] <= tmax):\n            s = (y[i] - y[i-1]) / dt\n            best = s if best is None else max(best, s)\n    return best\n\n\ndef time_above_threshold_s(df_tc, thr):\n    \"\"\"Calculate time above threshold for a single TC\"\"\"\n    g = df_tc.sort_values(\"time_s\")\n    t = g[\"time_s\"].to_numpy(dtype=float)\n    y = g[\"temp_c\"].to_numpy(dtype=float)\n    total = 0.0\n    for i in range(1, len(g)):\n        t0, t1 = t[i-1], t[i]\n        y0, y1 = y[i-1], y[i]\n        if t1 <= t0:\n            continue\n        if y0 > thr and y1 > thr:\n            total += (t1 - t0)\n            continue\n        crosses = (y0 <= thr < y1) or (y1 <= thr < y0)\n        if crosses and (y1 != y0):\n            frac = (thr - y0) / (y1 - y0)\n            tcross = t0 + frac * (t1 - t0)\n            if y0 <= thr and y1 > thr:\n                total += (t1 - tcross)\n            else:\n                total += (tcross - t0)\n    return total\n\n\ndef q01_max_preheat_ramp():\n    \"\"\"Q1: Calculate maximum preheat ramp rate for each run\"\"\"\n    print(\"Q1: Calculating maximum preheat ramp rate...\")\n\n    runs = pd.read_csv(os.path.join(DATA_DIR, \"mes_log.csv\"))\n    tc = pd.read_csv(os.path.join(DATA_DIR, \"thermocouples.csv\"))\n\n    runs[\"run_id\"] = runs[\"run_id\"].astype(str)\n    tc[\"run_id\"] = tc[\"run_id\"].astype(str)\n    tc[\"tc_id\"] = tc[\"tc_id\"].astype(str)\n\n    tc = tc.sort_values([\"run_id\", \"tc_id\", \"time_s\"], kind=\"mergesort\")\n\n    tmin = CFG[\"preheat_region\"][\"tmin\"]\n    tmax = CFG[\"preheat_region\"][\"tmax\"]\n    ramp_limit = CFG[\"ramp_limit_c_per_s\"]\n\n    # Calculate max ramp for each TC in each run\n    results_by_run = {}\n\n    for run_id in sorted(runs[\"run_id\"].unique()):\n        tc_run = tc[tc[\"run_id\"] == run_id]\n\n        if len(tc_run) == 0:\n            continue\n\n        max_ramp_tc = None\n        max_ramp_value = None\n\n        for tc_id in sorted(tc_run[\"tc_id\"].unique()):\n            tc_data = tc_run[tc_run[\"tc_id\"] == tc_id]\n            ramp = max_slope_in_temp_band(tc_data, tmin, tmax)\n\n            if ramp is not None:\n                if max_ramp_value is None or ramp > max_ramp_value:\n                    max_ramp_value = ramp\n                    max_ramp_tc = tc_id\n                elif ramp == max_ramp_value and (max_ramp_tc is None or tc_id < max_ramp_tc):\n                    # Tie-breaker: smallest tc_id\n                    max_ramp_tc = tc_id\n\n        if max_ramp_value is not None:\n            results_by_run[run_id] = {\n                \"tc_id\": max_ramp_tc,\n                \"max_preheat_ramp_c_per_s\": round(max_ramp_value, 2)\n            }\n\n    # Find violating runs\n    violating_runs = []\n    for run_id, data in results_by_run.items():\n        if data[\"max_preheat_ramp_c_per_s\"] > ramp_limit:\n            violating_runs.append(run_id)\n\n    output = {\n        \"ramp_rate_limit_c_per_s\": ramp_limit,\n        \"violating_runs\": sorted(violating_runs),\n        \"max_ramp_by_run\": dict(sorted(results_by_run.items()))\n    }\n\n    os.makedirs(OUTPUT_DIR, exist_ok=True)\n    with open(os.path.join(OUTPUT_DIR, \"q01.json\"), \"w\") as f:\n        json.dump(output, f, indent=2)\n\n    print(f\"  Found {len(violating_runs)} violating runs out of {len(results_by_run)} total runs\")\n\n\ndef q02_time_above_liquidus():\n    \"\"\"Q2: Calculate time above liquidus for each run\"\"\"\n    print(\"Q2: Calculating time above liquidus (TAL)...\")\n\n    runs = pd.read_csv(os.path.join(DATA_DIR, \"mes_log.csv\"))\n    tc = pd.read_csv(os.path.join(DATA_DIR, \"thermocouples.csv\"))\n\n    runs[\"run_id\"] = runs[\"run_id\"].astype(str)\n    tc[\"run_id\"] = tc[\"run_id\"].astype(str)\n    tc[\"tc_id\"] = tc[\"tc_id\"].astype(str)\n\n    tc = tc.sort_values([\"run_id\", \"tc_id\", \"time_s\"], kind=\"mergesort\")\n\n    # Merge to get liquidus temperature for each run\n    run_liquidus = runs[[\"run_id\", \"solder_liquidus_c\"]].copy()\n\n    results = []\n\n    for _, run_row in run_liquidus.iterrows():\n        run_id = run_row[\"run_id\"]\n        liquidus = run_row[\"solder_liquidus_c\"]\n\n        tc_run = tc[tc[\"run_id\"] == run_id]\n\n        for tc_id in sorted(tc_run[\"tc_id\"].unique()):\n            tc_data = tc_run[tc_run[\"tc_id\"] == tc_id]\n            tal_s = time_above_threshold_s(tc_data, liquidus)\n\n            # Determine compliance\n            if CFG[\"tal_min_s\"] <= tal_s <= CFG[\"tal_max_s\"]:\n                status = \"compliant\"\n            else:\n                status = \"non-compliant\"\n\n            results.append({\n                \"run_id\": run_id,\n                \"tc_id\": tc_id,\n                \"tal_s\": round(tal_s, 2),\n                \"required_min_tal_s\": CFG[\"tal_min_s\"],\n                \"required_max_tal_s\": CFG[\"tal_max_s\"],\n                \"status\": status\n            })\n\n    # Sort by run_id then tc_id\n    results.sort(key=lambda x: (x[\"run_id\"], x[\"tc_id\"]))\n\n    os.makedirs(OUTPUT_DIR, exist_ok=True)\n    with open(os.path.join(OUTPUT_DIR, \"q02.json\"), \"w\") as f:\n        json.dump(results, f, indent=2)\n\n    compliant_count = sum(1 for r in results if r[\"status\"] == \"compliant\")\n    print(f\"  Found {compliant_count} compliant out of {len(results)} total TC measurements\")\n\n\ndef q03_peak_temperature():\n    \"\"\"Q3: Check peak temperature requirements\"\"\"\n    print(\"Q3: Checking peak temperature requirements...\")\n\n    runs = pd.read_csv(os.path.join(DATA_DIR, \"mes_log.csv\"))\n    tc = pd.read_csv(os.path.join(DATA_DIR, \"thermocouples.csv\"))\n\n    runs[\"run_id\"] = runs[\"run_id\"].astype(str)\n    tc[\"run_id\"] = tc[\"run_id\"].astype(str)\n    tc[\"tc_id\"] = tc[\"tc_id\"].astype(str)\n\n    tc = tc.sort_values([\"run_id\", \"tc_id\", \"time_s\"], kind=\"mergesort\")\n\n    # Get liquidus for each run\n    run_liquidus = runs[[\"run_id\", \"solder_liquidus_c\"]].set_index(\"run_id\").to_dict()[\"solder_liquidus_c\"]\n\n    min_peak_by_run = {}\n    failing_runs = []\n\n    for run_id in sorted(runs[\"run_id\"].unique()):\n        tc_run = tc[tc[\"run_id\"] == run_id]\n        liquidus = run_liquidus[run_id]\n        required_min_peak = liquidus + CFG[\"peak_margin_c\"]\n\n        # If no TC data, mark as failing\n        if len(tc_run) == 0:\n            failing_runs.append(run_id)\n            min_peak_by_run[run_id] = {\n                \"tc_id\": None,\n                \"peak_temp_c\": None,\n                \"required_min_peak_c\": round(required_min_peak, 2)\n            }\n            continue\n\n        # Find minimum peak across all TCs\n        min_peak_tc = None\n        min_peak_value = None\n\n        for tc_id in sorted(tc_run[\"tc_id\"].unique()):\n            tc_data = tc_run[tc_run[\"tc_id\"] == tc_id]\n            peak = tc_data[\"temp_c\"].max()\n\n            if min_peak_value is None or peak < min_peak_value:\n                min_peak_value = peak\n                min_peak_tc = tc_id\n            elif peak == min_peak_value and (min_peak_tc is None or tc_id < min_peak_tc):\n                # Tie-breaker: smallest tc_id\n                min_peak_tc = tc_id\n\n        min_peak_by_run[run_id] = {\n            \"tc_id\": min_peak_tc,\n            \"peak_temp_c\": round(min_peak_value, 2),\n            \"required_min_peak_c\": round(required_min_peak, 2)\n        }\n\n        # Check if failing\n        if min_peak_value < required_min_peak:\n            failing_runs.append(run_id)\n\n    output = {\n        \"failing_runs\": sorted(failing_runs),\n        \"min_peak_by_run\": dict(sorted(min_peak_by_run.items()))\n    }\n\n    os.makedirs(OUTPUT_DIR, exist_ok=True)\n    with open(os.path.join(OUTPUT_DIR, \"q03.json\"), \"w\") as f:\n        json.dump(output, f, indent=2)\n\n    print(f\"  Found {len(failing_runs)} failing runs out of {len(min_peak_by_run)} total runs\")\n\n\ndef q04_conveyor_speed():\n    \"\"\"Q4: Check conveyor speed feasibility\"\"\"\n    print(\"Q4: Checking conveyor speed feasibility...\")\n\n    runs = pd.read_csv(os.path.join(DATA_DIR, \"mes_log.csv\"))\n\n    runs[\"run_id\"] = runs[\"run_id\"].astype(str)\n    runs = runs.sort_values(\"run_id\", kind=\"mergesort\")\n\n    # From handbook: need to determine heated length and required dwell time\n    # The handbook mentions process speed = heated length / process dwell time\n    # TAL requirement is 30-60 seconds, which is the dwell in the reflow zone\n    # sufficient time for the TAL requirement (30-60s)\n\n    # The heated length depends on the oven model\n    # From the handbook product matrix:\n    # - 8Z ovens (8Z_Convection, 8Z_IR+Conv): ~96 inches = 243.84 cm\n    # - 10Z ovens (10Z_Convection): ~114-148 inches = ~290-376 cm\n\n    # Let's use a simplified approach: check if the actual speed is within feasible range\n    # based on the oven heated length and required TAL dwell time\n\n    oven_heated_length = {\n        \"8Z_Convection\": 243.84,  # 96 inches in cm\n        \"8Z_IR+Conv\": 243.84,\n        \"10Z_Convection\": 289.56,  # 114 inches in cm\n    }\n\n    results = []\n\n    for _, run_row in runs.iterrows():\n        run_id = run_row[\"run_id\"]\n        oven_model = run_row[\"oven_model\"]\n        actual_speed_cm_min = run_row[\"conveyor_speed_cm_min\"]\n\n        # Get heated length for this oven model\n        heated_len_cm = oven_heated_length.get(oven_model, 243.84)  # Default to 8Z\n\n        # Calculate minimum required speed based on maximum dwell time (TAL max = 60s)\n        # Get yield summary for each run\n    yield_data = defects[defects[\"inspection_stage\"] == \"SUMMARY\"].copy()\n    yield_data = yield_data[[\"run_id\", \"fp_yield_pct\"]].drop_duplicates()\n\n    # Merge with runs\n    runs_with_yield = runs.merge(yield_data, on=\"run_id\", how=\"left\")\n\n    # Group by board_family\n    results = []\n\n    for board_family in sorted(runs_with_yield[\"board_family\"].unique()):\n        family_runs = runs_with_yield[runs_with_yield[\"board_family\"] == board_family].copy()\n\n        # Rank by:\n        # 1. Higher yield (quality)\n        # 2. Higher conveyor speed (efficiency)\n        # 3. Lower run_id (tie-breaker)\n\n        family_runs[\"rank_yield\"] = -family_runs[\"fp_yield_pct\"].fillna(0)\n        family_runs[\"rank_speed\"] = -family_runs[\"conveyor_speed_cm_min\"]\n        family_runs[\"rank_id\"] = family_runs[\"run_id\"]\n\n        family_runs = family_runs.sort_values(\n            [\"rank_yield\", \"rank_speed\", \"rank_id\"],\n            kind=\"mergesort\"\n        )\n\n        best_run = family_runs.iloc[0][\"run_id\"]\n        runner_up_runs = family_runs.iloc[1:][\"run_id\"].tolist()\n\n        results.append({\n            \"board_family\": board_family,\n            \"best_run_id\": best_run,\n            \"runner_up_run_ids\": runner_up_runs\n        })\n\n    # Sort by board_family\n    results.sort(key=lambda x: x[\"board_family\"])\n\n    os.makedirs(OUTPUT_DIR, exist_ok=True)\n    with open(os.path.join(OUTPUT_DIR, \"q05.json\"), \"w\") as f:\n        json.dump(results, f, indent=2)\n\n    print(f\"  Selected best runs for {len(results)} board families\")\n\n\nif __name__ == \"__main__\":\n    print(\"=\" * 60)\n    print(\"Reflow Profile Compliance Analysis\")\n    print(\"=\" * 60)\n    print()\n    print(\"Handbook-based configuration:\")\n    print(f\"  Preheat region: {CFG['preheat_region']['tmin']}-{CFG['preheat_region']['tmax']}°C\")\n    print(f\"  Max ramp rate: {CFG['ramp_limit_c_per_s']}°C/s\")\n    print(f\"  TAL window: {CFG['tal_min_s']}-{CFG['tal_max_s']}s\")\n    print(f\"  Peak margin above liquidus: {CFG['peak_margin_c']}°C\")\n    print()\n\n    q01_max_preheat_ramp()\n    print()\n    q02_time_above_liquidus()\n    print()\n    q03_peak_temperature()\n    print()\n    q04_conveyor_speed()\n    print()\n    q05_best_run_per_family()\n    print()\n    print(\"=\" * 60)\n    print(\"All analyses complete!\")\n    print(\"=\" * 60)\n","structuredPatch":[{"oldStart":272,"oldLines":23,"newStart":272,"newLines":21,"lines":["     runs[\"run_id\"] = runs[\"run_id\"].astype(str)","     runs = runs.sort_values(\"run_id\", kind=\"mergesort\")"," ","-    # Therefore: Dwell Time = Heated Length / Process Speed (converted to cm/s)\n    #\n    # The thermal dwell requirement based on TAL is 30-60 seconds\n    # However, TAL is only the time above liquidus, not total dwell\n    # Total dwell through the oven would be much longer\n    #\n    # For feasibility, the speed must ensure minimum TAL can be achieved\n    # This means: Speed_max = (Heated_Length / Min_TAL) * 60\n    # Speed must be <= Speed_max to allow sufficient time above liquidus\n    #\n    # Given typical profiles where TAL is a fraction of total dwell,\n    # and preheat region is 100-150°C, we estimate TAL zone is ~1/3 of heated length\n    # Conservative: assume TAL zone = heated_length / 2\n\n    # The heated length depends on the oven model\n    # From the handbook product matrix:\n    # - 8Z ovens (8Z_Convection, 8Z_IR+Conv): ~96 inches = 243.84 cm\n    # - 10Z ovens (10Z_Convection): ~114 inches = 289.56 cm\n\n    oven_heated_length = {\n        \"8Z_Convection\": 243.84,  # 96 inches in cm\n        \"8Z_IR+Conv\": 243.84,\n        \"10Z_Convection\": 289.56,  # Calculate maximum allowable speed to achieve minimum TAL (30s)\n        # Speed_max (cm/min) = (TAL_zone_length_cm / min_TAL_s) * 60\n        min_tal_s = CFG[\"tal_min_s\"]\n        max_allowable_speed_cm_min = (tal_zone_length_cm / min_tal_s) * 60\n\n        # For actual feasibility, use this as minimum required speed\n        # (Board must pass through fast enough to not overheat in TAL zone)\n        required_min_speed_cm_min = (tal_zone_length_cm / CFG[\"tal_max_s\"]) * 60\n\n        # Peak must exceed liquidus by 20°C\n}\n\n\ndef max_slope_in_temp_band(df_tc, tmin, tmax):\n    \"\"\"Calculate maximum slope within temperature band for a single TC\"\"\"\n    g = df_tc.sort_values(\"time_s\")\n    t = g[\"time_s\"].to_numpy(dtype=float)\n    y = g[\"temp_c\"].to_numpy(dtype=float)\n    best = None\n    for i in range(1, len(g)):\n        dt = t[i] - t[i-1]\n        if dt <= 0:\n            continue\n        # Both endpoints must be within the band\n        if (tmin <= y[i-1] <= tmax) and (tmin <= y[i] <= tmax):\n            s = (y[i] - y[i-1]) / dt\n            best = s if best is None else max(best, s)\n    return best\n\n\ndef time_above_threshold_s(df_tc, thr):\n    \"\"\"Calculate time above threshold for a single TC\"\"\"\n    g = df_tc.sort_values(\"time_s\")\n    t = g[\"time_s\"].to_numpy(dtype=float)\n    y = g[\"temp_c\"].to_numpy(dtype=float)\n    total = 0.0\n    for i in range(1, len(g)):\n        t0, t1 = t[i-1], t[i]\n        y0, y1 = y[i-1], y[i]\n        if t1 <= t0:\n            continue\n        if y0 > thr and y1 > thr:\n            total += (t1 - t0)\n            continue\n        crosses = (y0 <= thr < y1) or (y1 <= thr < y0)\n        if crosses and (y1 != y0):\n            frac = (thr - y0) / (y1 - y0)\n            tcross = t0 + frac * (t1 - t0)\n            if y0 <= thr and y1 > thr:\n                total += (t1 - tcross)\n            else:\n                total += (tcross - t0)\n    return total\n\n\ndef q01_max_preheat_ramp():\n    \"\"\"Q1: Calculate maximum preheat ramp rate for each run\"\"\"\n    print(\"Q1: Calculating maximum preheat ramp rate...\")\n\n    runs = pd.read_csv(os.path.join(DATA_DIR, \"mes_log.csv\"))\n    tc = pd.read_csv(os.path.join(DATA_DIR, \"thermocouples.csv\"))\n\n    runs[\"run_id\"] = runs[\"run_id\"].astype(str)\n    tc[\"run_id\"] = tc[\"run_id\"].astype(str)\n    tc[\"tc_id\"] = tc[\"tc_id\"].astype(str)\n\n    tc = tc.sort_values([\"run_id\", \"tc_id\", \"time_s\"], kind=\"mergesort\")\n\n    tmin = CFG[\"preheat_region\"][\"tmin\"]\n    tmax = CFG[\"preheat_region\"][\"tmax\"]\n    ramp_limit = CFG[\"ramp_limit_c_per_s\"]\n\n    # Calculate max ramp for each TC in each run\n    results_by_run = {}\n\n    for run_id in sorted(runs[\"run_id\"].unique()):\n        tc_run = tc[tc[\"run_id\"] == run_id]\n\n        if len(tc_run) == 0:\n            continue\n\n        max_ramp_tc = None\n        max_ramp_value = None\n\n        for tc_id in sorted(tc_run[\"tc_id\"].unique()):\n            tc_data = tc_run[tc_run[\"tc_id\"] == tc_id]\n            ramp = max_slope_in_temp_band(tc_data, tmin, tmax)\n\n            if ramp is not None:\n                if max_ramp_value is None or ramp > max_ramp_value:\n                    max_ramp_value = ramp\n                    max_ramp_tc = tc_id\n                elif ramp == max_ramp_value and (max_ramp_tc is None or tc_id < max_ramp_tc):\n                    # Tie-breaker: smallest tc_id\n                    max_ramp_tc = tc_id\n\n        if max_ramp_value is not None:\n            results_by_run[run_id] = {\n                \"tc_id\": max_ramp_tc,\n                \"max_preheat_ramp_c_per_s\": round(max_ramp_value, 2)\n            }\n\n    # Find violating runs\n    violating_runs = []\n    for run_id, data in results_by_run.items():\n        if data[\"max_preheat_ramp_c_per_s\"] > ramp_limit:\n            violating_runs.append(run_id)\n\n    output = {\n        \"ramp_rate_limit_c_per_s\": ramp_limit,\n        \"violating_runs\": sorted(violating_runs),\n        \"max_ramp_by_run\": dict(sorted(results_by_run.items()))\n    }\n\n    os.makedirs(OUTPUT_DIR, exist_ok=True)\n    with open(os.path.join(OUTPUT_DIR, \"q01.json\"), \"w\") as f:\n        json.dump(output, f, indent=2)\n\n    print(f\"  Found {len(violating_runs)} violating runs out of {len(results_by_run)} total runs\")\n\n\ndef q02_time_above_liquidus():\n    \"\"\"Q2: Calculate time above liquidus for each run\"\"\"\n    print(\"Q2: Calculating time above liquidus (TAL)...\")\n\n    runs = pd.read_csv(os.path.join(DATA_DIR, \"mes_log.csv\"))\n    tc = pd.read_csv(os.path.join(DATA_DIR, \"thermocouples.csv\"))\n\n    runs[\"run_id\"] = runs[\"run_id\"].astype(str)\n    tc[\"run_id\"] = tc[\"run_id\"].astype(str)\n    tc[\"tc_id\"] = tc[\"tc_id\"].astype(str)\n\n    tc = tc.sort_values([\"run_id\", \"tc_id\", \"time_s\"], kind=\"mergesort\")\n\n    # Merge to get liquidus temperature for each run\n    run_liquidus = runs[[\"run_id\", \"solder_liquidus_c\"]].copy()\n\n    results = []\n\n    for _, run_row in run_liquidus.iterrows():\n        run_id = run_row[\"run_id\"]\n        liquidus = run_row[\"solder_liquidus_c\"]\n\n        tc_run = tc[tc[\"run_id\"] == run_id]\n\n        for tc_id in sorted(tc_run[\"tc_id\"].unique()):\n            tc_data = tc_run[tc_run[\"tc_id\"] == tc_id]\n            tal_s = time_above_threshold_s(tc_data, liquidus)\n\n            # Determine compliance\n            if CFG[\"tal_min_s\"] <= tal_s <= CFG[\"tal_max_s\"]:\n                status = \"compliant\"\n            else:\n                status = \"non-compliant\"\n\n            results.append({\n                \"run_id\": run_id,\n                \"tc_id\": tc_id,\n                \"tal_s\": round(tal_s, 2),\n                \"required_min_tal_s\": CFG[\"tal_min_s\"],\n                \"required_max_tal_s\": CFG[\"tal_max_s\"],\n                \"status\": status\n            })\n\n    # Sort by run_id then tc_id\n    results.sort(key=lambda x: (x[\"run_id\"], x[\"tc_id\"]))\n\n    os.makedirs(OUTPUT_DIR, exist_ok=True)\n    with open(os.path.join(OUTPUT_DIR, \"q02.json\"), \"w\") as f:\n        json.dump(results, f, indent=2)\n\n    compliant_count = sum(1 for r in results if r[\"status\"] == \"compliant\")\n    print(f\"  Found {compliant_count} compliant out of {len(results)} total TC measurements\")\n\n\ndef q03_peak_temperature():\n    \"\"\"Q3: Check peak temperature requirements\"\"\"\n    print(\"Q3: Checking peak temperature requirements...\")\n\n    runs = pd.read_csv(os.path.join(DATA_DIR, \"mes_log.csv\"))\n    tc = pd.read_csv(os.path.join(DATA_DIR, \"thermocouples.csv\"))\n\n    runs[\"run_id\"] = runs[\"run_id\"].astype(str)\n    tc[\"run_id\"] = tc[\"run_id\"].astype(str)\n    tc[\"tc_id\"] = tc[\"tc_id\"].astype(str)\n\n    tc = tc.sort_values([\"run_id\", \"tc_id\", \"time_s\"], kind=\"mergesort\")\n\n    # Get liquidus for each run\n    run_liquidus = runs[[\"run_id\", \"solder_liquidus_c\"]].set_index(\"run_id\").to_dict()[\"solder_liquidus_c\"]\n\n    min_peak_by_run = {}\n    failing_runs = []\n\n    for run_id in sorted(runs[\"run_id\"].unique()):\n        tc_run = tc[tc[\"run_id\"] == run_id]\n        liquidus = run_liquidus[run_id]\n        required_min_peak = liquidus + CFG[\"peak_margin_c\"]\n\n        # If no TC data, mark as failing\n        if len(tc_run) == 0:\n            failing_runs.append(run_id)\n            min_peak_by_run[run_id] = {\n                \"tc_id\": None,\n                \"peak_temp_c\": None,\n                \"required_min_peak_c\": round(required_min_peak, 2)\n            }\n            continue\n\n        # Find minimum peak across all TCs\n        min_peak_tc = None\n        min_peak_value = None\n\n        for tc_id in sorted(tc_run[\"tc_id\"].unique()):\n            tc_data = tc_run[tc_run[\"tc_id\"] == tc_id]\n            peak = tc_data[\"temp_c\"].max()\n\n            if min_peak_value is None or peak < min_peak_value:\n                min_peak_value = peak\n                min_peak_tc = tc_id\n            elif peak == min_peak_value and (min_peak_tc is None or tc_id < min_peak_tc):\n                # Tie-breaker: smallest tc_id\n                min_peak_tc = tc_id\n\n        min_peak_by_run[run_id] = {\n            \"tc_id\": min_peak_tc,\n            \"peak_temp_c\": round(min_peak_value, 2),\n            \"required_min_peak_c\": round(required_min_peak, 2)\n        }\n\n        # Check if failing\n        if min_peak_value < required_min_peak:\n            failing_runs.append(run_id)\n\n    output = {\n        \"failing_runs\": sorted(failing_runs),\n        \"min_peak_by_run\": dict(sorted(min_peak_by_run.items()))\n    }\n\n    os.makedirs(OUTPUT_DIR, exist_ok=True)\n    with open(os.path.join(OUTPUT_DIR, \"q03.json\"), \"w\") as f:\n        json.dump(output, f, indent=2)\n\n    print(f\"  Found {len(failing_runs)} failing runs out of {len(min_peak_by_run)} total runs\")\n\n\ndef q04_conveyor_speed():\n    \"\"\"Q4: Check conveyor speed feasibility\"\"\"\n    print(\"Q4: Checking conveyor speed feasibility...\")\n\n    runs = pd.read_csv(os.path.join(DATA_DIR, \"mes_log.csv\"))\n\n    runs[\"run_id\"] = runs[\"run_id\"].astype(str)\n    runs = runs.sort_values(\"run_id\", kind=\"mergesort\")\n\n    # From handbook: Process Speed = Heated Length / Process Dwell Time\n    # Therefore: Process Dwell Time = Heated Length / Process Speed\n    #\n    # The TAL requirement is 30-60 seconds minimum dwell time in reflow\n    # The conveyor speed must be slow enough to achieve minimum dwell\n    # But fast enough to not exceed maximum dwell\n    #\n    # Maximum feasible speed: Speed_max = (Heated_Length / Min_Dwell) * 60\n    # Minimum feasible speed: Speed_min = (Heated_Length / Max_Dwell) * 60\n\n    # The heated length depends on the oven model\n    # From the handbook product matrix:\n    # - 8Z ovens (8Z_Convection, 8Z_IR+Conv): ~96 inches = 243.84 cm\n    # - 10Z ovens (10Z_Convection): ~114 inches = 289.56 cm\n\n    oven_heated_length = {\n        \"8Z_Convection\": 243.84,  # 96 inches in cm\n        \"8Z_IR+Conv\": 243.84,\n        \"10Z_Convection\": 289.56,  # 114 inches in cm\n    }\n\n    results = []\n\n    for _, run_row in runs.iterrows():\n        run_id = run_row[\"run_id\"]\n        oven_model = run_row[\"oven_model\"]\n        actual_speed_cm_min = run_row[\"conveyor_speed_cm_min\"]\n\n        # Get heated length for this oven model\n        heated_len_cm = oven_heated_length.get(oven_model, 243.84)  # Default to 8Z\n\n        # Calculate minimum required speed to not exceed max dwell (60s)\n        # If speed is too slow, dwell time will be too long\n        min_dwell_s = CFG[\"tal_min_s\"]\n        required_min_speed_cm_min = (heated_len_cm / min_dwell_s) * 60\n\n        # Check if meets requirement\n        # The actual speed must be >= minimum speed to ensure dwell doesn't exceed limits\n        meets = actual_speed_cm_min >= required_min_speed_cm_min\n\n        results.append({\n            \"run_id\": run_id,\n            \"required_min_speed_cm_min\": round(required_min_speed_cm_min, 2),\n            \"actual_speed_cm_min\": round(actual_speed_cm_min, 2),\n            \"meets\": meets\n        })\n\n    os.makedirs(OUTPUT_DIR, exist_ok=True)\n    with open(os.path.join(OUTPUT_DIR, \"q04.json\"), \"w\") as f:\n        json.dump(results, f, indent=2)\n\n    meets_count = sum(1 for r in results if r[\"meets\"])\n    print(f\"  Found {meets_count} runs meeting speed requirements out of {len(results)} total runs\")\n\n\ndef q05_best_run_per_family():\n    \"\"\"Q5: Select best run per board family\"\"\"\n    print(\"Q5: Selecting best run per board family...\")\n\n    runs = pd.read_csv(os.path.join(DATA_DIR, \"mes_log.csv\"))\n    defects = pd.read_csv(os.path.join(DATA_DIR, \"test_defects.csv\"))\n\n    runs[\"run_id\"] = runs[\"run_id\"].astype(str)\n    defects[\"run_id\"] = defects[\"run_id\"].astype(str)\n\n    # Get yield summary for each run\n    yield_data = defects[defects[\"inspection_stage\"] == \"SUMMARY\"].copy()\n    yield_data = yield_data[[\"run_id\", \"fp_yield_pct\"]].drop_duplicates()\n\n    # Merge with runs\n    runs_with_yield = runs.merge(yield_data, on=\"run_id\", how=\"left\")\n\n    # Group by board_family\n    results = []\n\n    for board_family in sorted(runs_with_yield[\"board_family\"].unique()):\n        family_runs = runs_with_yield[runs_with_yield[\"board_family\"] == board_family].copy()\n\n        # Rank by:\n        # 1. Higher yield (quality)\n        # 2. Higher conveyor speed (efficiency)\n        # 3. Lower run_id (tie-breaker)\n\n        family_runs[\"rank_yield\"] = -family_runs[\"fp_yield_pct\"].fillna(0)\n        family_runs[\"rank_speed\"] = -family_runs[\"conveyor_speed_cm_min\"]\n        family_runs[\"rank_id\"] = family_runs[\"run_id\"]\n\n        family_runs = family_runs.sort_values(\n            [\"rank_yield\", \"rank_speed\", \"rank_id\"],\n            kind=\"mergesort\"\n        )\n\n        best_run = family_runs.iloc[0][\"run_id\"]\n        runner_up_runs = family_runs.iloc[1:][\"run_id\"].tolist()\n\n        results.append({\n            \"board_family\": board_family,\n            \"best_run_id\": best_run,\n            \"runner_up_run_ids\": runner_up_runs\n        })\n\n    # Sort by board_family\n    results.sort(key=lambda x: x[\"board_family\"])\n\n    os.makedirs(OUTPUT_DIR, exist_ok=True)\n    with open(os.path.join(OUTPUT_DIR, \"q05.json\"), \"w\") as f:\n        json.dump(results, f, indent=2)\n\n    print(f\"  Selected best runs for {len(results)} board families\")\n\n\nif __name__ == \"__main__\":\n    print(\"=\" * 60)\n    print(\"Reflow Profile Compliance Analysis\")\n    print(\"=\" * 60)\n    print()\n    print(\"Handbook-based configuration:\")\n    print(f\"  Preheat region: {CFG['preheat_region']['tmin']}-{CFG['preheat_region']['tmax']}°C\")\n    print(f\"  Max ramp rate: {CFG['ramp_limit_c_per_s']}°C/s\")\n    print(f\"  TAL window: {CFG['tal_min_s']}-{CFG['tal_max_s']}s\")\n    print(f\"  Peak margin above liquidus: {CFG['peak_margin_c']}°C\")\n    print()\n\n    q01_max_preheat_ramp()\n    print()\n    q02_time_above_liquidus()\n    print()\n    q03_peak_temperature()\n    print()\n    q04_conveyor_speed()\n    print()\n    q05_best_run_per_family()\n    print()\n    print(\"=\" * 60)\n    print(\"All analyses complete!\")\n    print(\"=\" * 60)\n","structuredPatch":[{"oldStart":273,"oldLines":14,"newStart":273,"newLines":19,"lines":["     runs = runs.sort_values(\"run_id\", kind=\"mergesort\")"," ","     # Speed must be >= min to avoid excessive dwell\n        required_min_speed_cm_min = (heated_len_cm / max_dwell_s) * 60\n\n        # Check if meets requirement\n        meets = actual_speed_cm_min >= required_min_speed_cm_min","originalFile":"#!/usr/bin/env python3\n\"\"\"\nReflow Profile Compliance Analysis\nBased on handbook definitions and thermocouple/MES data\n\"\"\"\n\nimport pandas as pd\nimport numpy as np\nimport json\nimport os\n\nDATA_DIR = \"/app/data\"\nOUTPUT_DIR = \"/app/output\"\n\n# Handbook-based configuration\n# From handbook page 5:\n# \"The most critical parameter in the preheat section is to minimize\n#  the rate of rise to less than 2°C/second\"\n# \"the preheat section, the goal is to fully preheat the SMT assembly\n#  to temperatures generally between 100°C to 150°C\"\n# \"wetting time is normally 30 to 60 seconds for most pastes\"\n# \"This temperature must be exceeded by approximately 20°C to ensure quality reflow\"\n\nCFG = {\n    \"preheat_region\": {\"type\": \"temp_band\", \"tmin\": 100, \"tmax\": 150},\n    \"ramp_limit_c_per_s\": 2.0,\n    \"tal_min_s\": 30,\n    \"tal_max_s\": 60,\n    \"peak_margin_c\": 20,  # Peak must exceed liquidus by 20°C\n}\n\n\ndef max_slope_in_temp_band(df_tc, tmin, tmax):\n    \"\"\"Calculate maximum slope within temperature band for a single TC\"\"\"\n    g = df_tc.sort_values(\"time_s\")\n    t = g[\"time_s\"].to_numpy(dtype=float)\n    y = g[\"temp_c\"].to_numpy(dtype=float)\n    best = None\n    for i in range(1, len(g)):\n        dt = t[i] - t[i-1]\n        if dt <= 0:\n            continue\n        # Both endpoints must be within the band\n        if (tmin <= y[i-1] <= tmax) and (tmin <= y[i] <= tmax):\n            s = (y[i] - y[i-1]) / dt\n            best = s if best is None else max(best, s)\n    return best\n\n\ndef time_above_threshold_s(df_tc, thr):\n    \"\"\"Calculate time above threshold for a single TC\"\"\"\n    g = df_tc.sort_values(\"time_s\")\n    t = g[\"time_s\"].to_numpy(dtype=float)\n    y = g[\"temp_c\"].to_numpy(dtype=float)\n    total = 0.0\n    for i in range(1, len(g)):\n        t0, t1 = t[i-1], t[i]\n        y0, y1 = y[i-1], y[i]\n        if t1 <= t0:\n            continue\n        if y0 > thr and y1 > thr:\n            total += (t1 - t0)\n            continue\n        crosses = (y0 <= thr < y1) or (y1 <= thr < y0)\n        if crosses and (y1 != y0):\n            frac = (thr - y0) / (y1 - y0)\n            tcross = t0 + frac * (t1 - t0)\n            if y0 <= thr and y1 > thr:\n                total += (t1 - tcross)\n            else:\n                total += (tcross - t0)\n    return total\n\n\ndef q01_max_preheat_ramp():\n    \"\"\"Q1: Calculate maximum preheat ramp rate for each run\"\"\"\n    print(\"Q1: Calculating maximum preheat ramp rate...\")\n\n    runs = pd.read_csv(os.path.join(DATA_DIR, \"mes_log.csv\"))\n    tc = pd.read_csv(os.path.join(DATA_DIR, \"thermocouples.csv\"))\n\n    runs[\"run_id\"] = runs[\"run_id\"].astype(str)\n    tc[\"run_id\"] = tc[\"run_id\"].astype(str)\n    tc[\"tc_id\"] = tc[\"tc_id\"].astype(str)\n\n    tc = tc.sort_values([\"run_id\", \"tc_id\", \"time_s\"], kind=\"mergesort\")\n\n    tmin = CFG[\"preheat_region\"][\"tmin\"]\n    tmax = CFG[\"preheat_region\"][\"tmax\"]\n    ramp_limit = CFG[\"ramp_limit_c_per_s\"]\n\n    # Calculate max ramp for each TC in each run\n    results_by_run = {}\n\n    for run_id in sorted(runs[\"run_id\"].unique()):\n        tc_run = tc[tc[\"run_id\"] == run_id]\n\n        if len(tc_run) == 0:\n            continue\n\n        max_ramp_tc = None\n        max_ramp_value = None\n\n        for tc_id in sorted(tc_run[\"tc_id\"].unique()):\n            tc_data = tc_run[tc_run[\"tc_id\"] == tc_id]\n            ramp = max_slope_in_temp_band(tc_data, tmin, tmax)\n\n            if ramp is not None:\n                if max_ramp_value is None or ramp > max_ramp_value:\n                    max_ramp_value = ramp\n                    max_ramp_tc = tc_id\n                elif ramp == max_ramp_value and (max_ramp_tc is None or tc_id < max_ramp_tc):\n                    # Tie-breaker: smallest tc_id\n                    max_ramp_tc = tc_id\n\n        if max_ramp_value is not None:\n            results_by_run[run_id] = {\n                \"tc_id\": max_ramp_tc,\n                \"max_preheat_ramp_c_per_s\": round(max_ramp_value, 2)\n            }\n\n    # Find violating runs\n    violating_runs = []\n    for run_id, data in results_by_run.items():\n        if data[\"max_preheat_ramp_c_per_s\"] > ramp_limit:\n            violating_runs.append(run_id)\n\n    output = {\n        \"ramp_rate_limit_c_per_s\": ramp_limit,\n        \"violating_runs\": sorted(violating_runs),\n        \"max_ramp_by_run\": dict(sorted(results_by_run.items()))\n    }\n\n    os.makedirs(OUTPUT_DIR, exist_ok=True)\n    with open(os.path.join(OUTPUT_DIR, \"q01.json\"), \"w\") as f:\n        json.dump(output, f, indent=2)\n\n    print(f\"  Found {len(violating_runs)} violating runs out of {len(results_by_run)} total runs\")\n\n\ndef q02_time_above_liquidus():\n    \"\"\"Q2: Calculate time above liquidus for each run\"\"\"\n    print(\"Q2: Calculating time above liquidus (TAL)...\")\n\n    runs = pd.read_csv(os.path.join(DATA_DIR, \"mes_log.csv\"))\n    tc = pd.read_csv(os.path.join(DATA_DIR, \"thermocouples.csv\"))\n\n    runs[\"run_id\"] = runs[\"run_id\"].astype(str)\n    tc[\"run_id\"] = tc[\"run_id\"].astype(str)\n    tc[\"tc_id\"] = tc[\"tc_id\"].astype(str)\n\n    tc = tc.sort_values([\"run_id\", \"tc_id\", \"time_s\"], kind=\"mergesort\")\n\n    # Merge to get liquidus temperature for each run\n    run_liquidus = runs[[\"run_id\", \"solder_liquidus_c\"]].copy()\n\n    results = []\n\n    for _, run_row in run_liquidus.iterrows():\n        run_id = run_row[\"run_id\"]\n        liquidus = run_row[\"solder_liquidus_c\"]\n\n        tc_run = tc[tc[\"run_id\"] == run_id]\n\n        for tc_id in sorted(tc_run[\"tc_id\"].unique()):\n            tc_data = tc_run[tc_run[\"tc_id\"] == tc_id]\n            tal_s = time_above_threshold_s(tc_data, liquidus)\n\n            # Determine compliance\n            if CFG[\"tal_min_s\"] <= tal_s <= CFG[\"tal_max_s\"]:\n                status = \"compliant\"\n            else:\n                status = \"non-compliant\"\n\n            results.append({\n                \"run_id\": run_id,\n                \"tc_id\": tc_id,\n                \"tal_s\": round(tal_s, 2),\n                \"required_min_tal_s\": CFG[\"tal_min_s\"],\n                \"required_max_tal_s\": CFG[\"tal_max_s\"],\n                \"status\": status\n            })\n\n    # Sort by run_id then tc_id\n    results.sort(key=lambda x: (x[\"run_id\"], x[\"tc_id\"]))\n\n    os.makedirs(OUTPUT_DIR, exist_ok=True)\n    with open(os.path.join(OUTPUT_DIR, \"q02.json\"), \"w\") as f:\n        json.dump(results, f, indent=2)\n\n    compliant_count = sum(1 for r in results if r[\"status\"] == \"compliant\")\n    print(f\"  Found {compliant_count} compliant out of {len(results)} total TC measurements\")\n\n\ndef q03_peak_temperature():\n    \"\"\"Q3: Check peak temperature requirements\"\"\"\n    print(\"Q3: Checking peak temperature requirements...\")\n\n    runs = pd.read_csv(os.path.join(DATA_DIR, \"mes_log.csv\"))\n    tc = pd.read_csv(os.path.join(DATA_DIR, \"thermocouples.csv\"))\n\n    runs[\"run_id\"] = runs[\"run_id\"].astype(str)\n    tc[\"run_id\"] = tc[\"run_id\"].astype(str)\n    tc[\"tc_id\"] = tc[\"tc_id\"].astype(str)\n\n    tc = tc.sort_values([\"run_id\", \"tc_id\", \"time_s\"], kind=\"mergesort\")\n\n    # Get liquidus for each run\n    run_liquidus = runs[[\"run_id\", \"solder_liquidus_c\"]].set_index(\"run_id\").to_dict()[\"solder_liquidus_c\"]\n\n    min_peak_by_run = {}\n    failing_runs = []\n\n    for run_id in sorted(runs[\"run_id\"].unique()):\n        tc_run = tc[tc[\"run_id\"] == run_id]\n        liquidus = run_liquidus[run_id]\n        required_min_peak = liquidus + CFG[\"peak_margin_c\"]\n\n        # If no TC data, mark as failing\n        if len(tc_run) == 0:\n            failing_runs.append(run_id)\n            min_peak_by_run[run_id] = {\n                \"tc_id\": None,\n                \"peak_temp_c\": None,\n                \"required_min_peak_c\": round(required_min_peak, 2)\n            }\n            continue\n\n        # Find minimum peak across all TCs\n        min_peak_tc = None\n        min_peak_value = None\n\n        for tc_id in sorted(tc_run[\"tc_id\"].unique()):\n            tc_data = tc_run[tc_run[\"tc_id\"] == tc_id]\n            peak = tc_data[\"temp_c\"].max()\n\n            if min_peak_value is None or peak < min_peak_value:\n                min_peak_value = peak\n                min_peak_tc = tc_id\n            elif peak == min_peak_value and (min_peak_tc is None or tc_id < min_peak_tc):\n                # Tie-breaker: smallest tc_id\n                min_peak_tc = tc_id\n\n        min_peak_by_run[run_id] = {\n            \"tc_id\": min_peak_tc,\n            \"peak_temp_c\": round(min_peak_value, 2),\n            \"required_min_peak_c\": round(required_min_peak, 2)\n        }\n\n        # Check if failing\n        if min_peak_value < required_min_peak:\n            failing_runs.append(run_id)\n\n    output = {\n        \"failing_runs\": sorted(failing_runs),\n        \"min_peak_by_run\": dict(sorted(min_peak_by_run.items()))\n    }\n\n    os.makedirs(OUTPUT_DIR, exist_ok=True)\n    with open(os.path.join(OUTPUT_DIR, \"q03.json\"), \"w\") as f:\n        json.dump(output, f, indent=2)\n\n    print(f\"  Found {len(failing_runs)} failing runs out of {len(min_peak_by_run)} total runs\")\n\n\ndef q04_conveyor_speed():\n    \"\"\"Q4: Check conveyor speed feasibility\"\"\"\n    print(\"Q4: Checking conveyor speed feasibility...\")\n\n    runs = pd.read_csv(os.path.join(DATA_DIR, \"mes_log.csv\"))\n\n    runs[\"run_id\"] = runs[\"run_id\"].astype(str)\n    runs = runs.sort_values(\"run_id\", kind=\"mergesort\")\n\n    # From handbook: Process Speed = Heated Length / Process Dwell Time\n    # Therefore: Dwell Time = Heated Length / Process Speed (converted to cm/s)\n    #\n    # The thermal dwell requirement based on TAL is 30-60 seconds\n    # However, TAL is only the time above liquidus, not total dwell\n    # Total dwell through the oven would be much longer\n    #\n    # For feasibility, the speed must ensure minimum TAL can be achieved\n    # This means: Speed_max = (Heated_Length / Min_TAL) * 60\n    # Speed must be <= Speed_max to allow sufficient time above liquidus\n    #\n    # Given typical profiles where TAL is a fraction of total dwell,\n    # and preheat region is 100-150°C, we estimate TAL zone is ~1/3 of heated length\n    # Conservative: assume TAL zone = heated_length / 2\n\n    # The heated length depends on the oven model\n    # From the handbook product matrix:\n    # - 8Z ovens (8Z_Convection, 8Z_IR+Conv): ~96 inches = 243.84 cm\n    # - 10Z ovens (10Z_Convection): ~114 inches = 289.56 cm\n\n    oven_heated_length = {\n        \"8Z_Convection\": 243.84,  # 96 inches in cm\n        \"8Z_IR+Conv\": 243.84,\n        \"10Z_Convection\": 289.56,  # 114 inches in cm\n    }\n\n    results = []\n\n    for _, run_row in runs.iterrows():\n        run_id = run_row[\"run_id\"]\n        oven_model = run_row[\"oven_model\"]\n        actual_speed_cm_min = run_row[\"conveyor_speed_cm_min\"]\n        board_length_cm = run_row[\"board_length_cm\"]\n\n        # Get heated length for this oven model\n        heated_len_cm = oven_heated_length.get(oven_model, 243.84)  # Default to 8Z\n\n        # Estimate TAL zone length (conservative: 50% of heated length)\n        tal_zone_length_cm = heated_len_cm * 0.5\n\n        # Calculate maximum allowable speed to achieve minimum TAL (30s)\n        # Speed_max (cm/min) = (TAL_zone_length_cm / min_TAL_s) * 60\n        min_tal_s = CFG[\"tal_min_s\"]\n        max_allowable_speed_cm_min = (tal_zone_length_cm / min_tal_s) * 60\n\n        # For actual feasibility, use this as minimum required speed\n        # (Board must pass through fast enough to not overheat in TAL zone)\n        required_min_speed_cm_min = (tal_zone_length_cm / CFG[\"tal_max_s\"]) * 60\n\n        # Check if meets requirement: speed must be >= min to avoid excessive TAL\n        meets = actual_speed_cm_min >= required_min_speed_cm_min\n\n        results.append({\n            \"run_id\": run_id,\n            \"required_min_speed_cm_min\": round(required_min_speed_cm_min, 2),\n            \"actual_speed_cm_min\": round(actual_speed_cm_min, 2),\n            \"meets\": meets\n        })\n\n    os.makedirs(OUTPUT_DIR, exist_ok=True)\n    with open(os.path.join(OUTPUT_DIR, \"q04.json\"), \"w\") as f:\n        json.dump(results, f, indent=2)\n\n    meets_count = sum(1 for r in results if r[\"meets\"])\n    print(f\"  Found {meets_count} runs meeting speed requirements out of {len(results)} total runs\")\n\n\ndef q05_best_run_per_family():\n    \"\"\"Q5: Select best run per board family\"\"\"\n    print(\"Q5: Selecting best run per board family...\")\n\n    runs = pd.read_csv(os.path.join(DATA_DIR, \"mes_log.csv\"))\n    defects = pd.read_csv(os.path.join(DATA_DIR, \"test_defects.csv\"))\n\n    runs[\"run_id\"] = runs[\"run_id\"].astype(str)\n    defects[\"run_id\"] = defects[\"run_id\"].astype(str)\n\n    # Rank by:\n        # 1. Higher yield (quality)\n        # 2. Higher conveyor speed (efficiency)\n        # 3. Lower run_id (tie-breaker)\n\n        family_runs[\"rank_yield\"] = -family_runs[\"fp_yield_pct\"].fillna(0)\n        family_runs[\"rank_speed\"] = -family_runs[\"conveyor_speed_cm_min\"]\n        family_runs[\"rank_id\"] = family_runs[\"run_id\"]\n\n        family_runs = family_runs.sort_values(\n            [\"rank_yield\", \"rank_speed\", \"rank_id\"],\n            kind=\"mergesort\"\n        )\n\n        best_run = family_runs.iloc[0][\"run_id\"]\n        runner_up_runs = family_runs.iloc[1:][\"run_id\"].tolist()\n\n        results.append({\n            \"board_family\": board_family,\n            \"best_run_id\": best_run,\n            \"runner_up_run_ids\": runner_up_runs\n        })\n\n    # << 'EOF' > /app/ANALYSIS_SUMMARY.md\n# Reflow Profile Compliance Analysis Summary\n\n## Overview\nThis analysis evaluates reflow machine process compliance based on handbook specifications and actual production data (thermocouple sensors, MES logs, and defect data) for 60 production runs across 6 board families.\n\n## Handbook-Based Configuration\n\n### Key Parameters Extracted from Handbook\n1. **Preheat Region**: 100-150°C (Page 5: \"the preheat section, the goal is to fully preheat the SMT assembly to temperatures generally between 100°C to 150°C\")\n\n2. **Maximum Ramp Rate**: 2.0°C/s (Page 5: \"The most critical parameter in the preheat section is to minimize the rate of rise to less than 2°C/second\")\n\n3. **Time Above Liquidus (TAL)**: 30-60 seconds (Page 5: \"The wetting time is normally 30 to 60 seconds for most pastes\")\n\n4. **Peak Temperature Margin**: 20°C above liquidus (Page 5: \"This temperature must be exceeded by approximately 20°C to ensure quality reflow\")\n\n5. **Oven Heated Lengths** (from Product Matrix, Page 22):\n   - 8Z ovens (8Z_Convection, 8Z_IR+Conv): 96 inches = 243.84 cm\n   - 10Z ovens (10Z_Convection): 114 inches = 289.56 cm\n\n## Results Summary\n\n### Q1: Maximum Preheat Ramp Rate\n- **Total runs analyzed**: 60\n- **Violating runs**: 9 runs (15%)\n- **Limit**: 2.0°C/s\n- **Violating runs**: R0018, R0025, R0035, R0040, R0044, R0046, R0050, R0051, R0053\n\n**Analysis Method**:\n- For each thermocouple in each run, calculated max slope within 100-150°C preheat region\n- Selected maximum ramp across all thermocouples (worst-case scenario)\n- Used smallest TC ID as tie-breaker for equal values\n\n### Q2: Time Above Liquidus (TAL)\n- **Total TC measurements**: 360 (60 runs × 6 TCs per run average)\n- **Compliant measurements**: 47 (13%)\n- **TAL requirement**: 30-60 seconds\n\n**Analysis Method**:\n- For each thermocouple, calculated time above solder liquidus temperature using segment interpolation\n- Liquidus temperature obtained from MES log (solder_liquidus_c field)\n- Status: compliant if 30s ≤ TAL ≤ 60s, otherwise non-compliant\n\n**Key Finding**: Most runs show TAL values outside the target range, with many significantly exceeding 60s, indicating slower-than-optimal conveyor speeds.\n\n### Q3: Peak Temperature Requirements\n- **Total runs**: 60\n- **Failing runs**: 14 runs (23%)\n- **Requirement**: Peak ≥ Liquidus + 20°C\n\n**Failing runs**: R0004, R0005, R0008, R0009, R0011, R0013, R0014, R0018, R0024, R0025, R0032, R0033, R0040, R0046\n\n**Analysis Method**:\n- Found minimum peak temperature across all thermocouples for each run\n- Compared against required minimum (liquidus + 20°C)\n- Runs with no TC data automatically marked as failing\n\n### Q4: Conveyor Speed Feasibility\n- **Total runs**: 60\n- **Meeting requirements**: 59 runs (98.3%)\n- **Not meeting**: R0035 only\n\n**Analysis Method**:\n- Calculated minimum required speed to avoid excessive dwell time (>400s max)\n- Formula: Speed_min = (Heated_Length / Max_Dwell_Time) * 60\n- Used oven model to determine heated length\n- Speed is feasible if actual speed ≥ minimum required speed\n\n### Q5: Best Run Per Board Family\nSelected best run for each of 6 board families based on:\n1. **Primary criterion**: Highest yield (fp_yield_pct from defect data)\n2. **Secondary criterion**: Highest conveyor speed (efficiency)\n3. **Tie-breaker**: Lowest run_id\n\n**Results**:\n- **Compute_IO**: R0002 (100% yield, 75.1 cm/min)\n- **Industrial_CTRL**: R0022 (97.92% yield, 61.6 cm/min)\n- **Mobile_PWR**: R0007 (97.26% yield, 81.6 cm/min)\n- **RF_FrontEnd**: R0031 (97.4% yield, 40.9 cm/min)\n- **Sensor_Module**: R0026 (97.31% yield, 49.2 cm/min)\n- **Wearable**: R0050 (100% yield, 84.8 cm/min)\n\n## Technical Implementation Notes\n\n### Calculation Methods\n1. **Max Slope in Temperature Band**: Computed segment-by-segment slopes only where both endpoints fall within the specified temperature range\n2. **Time Above Threshold**: Used linear interpolation to find crossing points and accumulated time above liquidus\n3. **Peak Detection**: Simple maximum temperature across all samples for each thermocouple\n4. **Tie-breaking**: When multiple values were equal, selected smallest TC ID or run ID\n\n### Data Quality\n- All thermocouple data sorted by time before processing\n- Segments with dt ≤ 0 ignored (data quality check)\n- Null values handled appropriately in calculations\n\n## Output Files\n- `/app/output/q01.json`: Maximum preheat ramp rates by run\n- `/app/output/q02.json`: TAL measurements for all TCs\n- `/app/output/q03.json`: Peak temperature compliance by run\n- `/app/output/q04.json`: Conveyor speed feasibility by run\n- `/app/output/q05.json`: Best run selection by board family\n\n## Conclusions\n1. **Preheat ramp control** is generally good (85% compliance)\n2. **TAL compliance** is poor (13%), suggesting process optimization needed\n3. **Peak temperature** achievement is adequate (77% passing)\n4. **Conveyor speed** is almost always feasible (98%+)\n5. **Best runs** identified provide reference recipes for each board family\n\nEOF\ncat
